### Consequential moving-shot cue timeline (DESIGN_PROPOSAL example)

For every consequential walking/stop or camera-moving shot, return these cue rows populated as part of the shot plan. Use shot-local times within the owning shot's duration; actor and camera phases share that basis. Include applicable actor/camera onset, deceleration, stop and settle sub-intervals. In `Actor phase / contact / visibility`, state the visible foot-contact cue/window or mark it `hidden/out-of-frame` or `unverified`. In `Reveal / hold`, give reaction onset and a distinct hold start?end when used. For an action cut, pair out/in phases and their shot-local cue times here and in the existing 09 edit slot, keeping each cue within its owning final range. In the existing AI-handoff tool-support slot, write `UNKNOWN` until the actual endpoint control is checked; until then, camera direction remains a DESIGN_PROPOSAL. These are proposed values, not observations or universal gait timings.

| Local time (shot seconds) | Actor phase / contact / visibility | Camera phase | Reveal / hold | Cut-out �� cut-in phase |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| 0.00 | Walk begins; feet visible, no contact claim yet | Track begins with actor | Path revealed; no hold | ? |
| 0.00?1.20 | Approach; show foot plants/weight transfers only where visible in this frame | Track alongside; preserve spacing | Keep both actors readable | ? |
| 1.20?1.50 | Decelerate; final plant and transfer to supporting foot visible | Track decelerates | ? | ? |
| 1.50?1.85 | Stop; planted feet remain visible, body settles | Track stops, camera settles | Hold begins after settle | ? |
| 1.85?2.00 | Partner reaction begins after the stop | Locked | Reaction onset 1.85; hold not started | ? |
| 2.00?2.55 | Settled mutual eyeline | Locked | Reaction hold 2.00?2.55 | SH01 out 2.55: settled look �� SH02 in 0.00: reverse begins on matching eyeline |

# Text-only production handoffs

All time ranges below are **proposed final seconds**; each ends before the next begins. These are draft handoffs based on the seven briefs. No footage, audio, artwork, render, approval, or rights record was supplied. `source_in_s` and `source_out_s` are therefore N/A for every proposed cut; `playback_rate` is 1.0 unless an edit is later specified.

## Original F1 — STILLWATER, 12 seconds

**Product SSOT fields.** `product_id: STILLWATER`; `packaging shape: oval`; `material/color: sea-green glass`; `cap/closure: brushed silver, offset`; `label position/design: small ivory label, exact artwork pending`; `must-never-change: silhouette, glass hue, offset cap, label size and position`. Logo lettering is unverified until owner artwork arrives. The final close must use that artwork or a clearly blank proposal label.

**Shot and edit fields** (`shot_id`, `start_s–end_s`, `blocking`, `camera_motion`, `start_state → end_state`; hard cuts):

| Shot | Time | Action and cut cue |
|---|---:|---|
| SH01 | 0–2.5 | Adult’s right hand carries capped bottle into a stable mirrored-tray frame. Decelerate 1.7–2.1; base contacts tray 2.1–2.3; fingers release 2.3–2.5. Camera locked. |
| SH02 | 2.5–5.0 | Same right hand grips and lifts cap at 3.1–4.0. Cut only after cap separates; cap stays in that hand and remains off bottle. |
| SH03 | 5.0–7.5 | Left wrist enters; right hand holds bottle and depresses atomizer once at 6.1–6.5. Show nozzle direction and wrist contact result without implying a measured effect. |
| SH04 | 7.5–9.5 | Abstract green light softens around the stationary wrist and bottle. Camera eases toward bottle; light is an expressive layer, not a bodily transformation. |
| SH05 | 9.5–12 | Readable bottle close; hold label face 10.2–12. Cap remains removed, wrist outside the packshot. |

`copy`/edit window: **“Leave the hurry at the door.”** appears or is spoken at 0.5–3.8; no VO is assumed. Leave 10.2–12 clear for the bottle. `sound_id: N/A—no source audio; any music or spray cue remains a proposal`.

**State fields.** `object ID: bottle`; `change trigger: placement, cap removal, one spray`; `before → after: carried/capped/dry tray → standing/uncapped/tray unchanged, wrist visually sprayed`; `retention: cap stays off; no second spray or unexplained tray droplets`. In the mirror, preserve one coherent bottle silhouette and label orientation. Keep a controlled reflection highlight beside the label, never over its text. If the green effect masks the bottle, use a clean wrist-to-packshot hard cut.

**Evidence fields.** `claim_id CL-F1-01`, `statement: Leave the hurry at the door`, `kind: fiction/creative line`, `source_id: F1 brief`, `limitations: metaphor; no calm, health, or performance benefit`, `status: fiction`. Commercial rights for artwork, talent, music, and any supplied product design remain unverified. Shopping CTA: **N/A**, because this is a concept without a required purchase action.

## Original H1 — Lantern Quay, 16 seconds

**Location and actor handoff.** Master path: south vestibule entrance → east turn at brass lantern → curtain threshold → tea room → view through north window → return through curtain toward vestibule. Keep lantern on the guest’s turning side and north window ahead in the tea room. Guest’s playable goal is to find the sheltered room; host’s is to welcome and offer tea. The cup starts in the host’s hand and ends in the guest’s hand only after visible shared contact and host release.

| Shot | Time | `blocking` / `camera_motion` / cut phase |
|---|---:|---|
| SH01 | 0–3 | Wide south entrance: guest walks in, notices lantern at 1.5, slows 2.2, plants and turns east 2.5–3. Camera tracks then settles. |
| SH02 | 3–5 | Guest POV past the brass lantern to curtain; eastward motion continues through threshold. Cut out as hand parts curtain. |
| SH03 | 5–8.5 | Host reverse in tea room. Host sees guest enter, lifts warm cup 5.8, offers 6.4. Guest grips 7.1–7.5; host releases 7.6. Hold guest’s reaction 7.8–8.5. |
| SH04 | 8.5–11.5 | Guest POV north window. A **brief window wipe** at 9.4–10.4 reveals an intentionally impossible miniature garden, framed as fantasy. Return to the same window edge and ordinary garden view by 11.2. |
| SH05 | 11.5–16 | Host reaction to guest’s glance, then guest looks back to lantern/curtain and walks toward vestibule. Hold the recovered east–south orientation at 14.5–16. |

`eyeline`: guest looks north to window; host follows the guest’s gaze before the fantasy insert. `start_state → end_state`: cup host-held → guest-held; curtain parted → falls closed; no implied teleport. `transition/duration_s`: window wipe 1.0; other cuts hard/0. Alternative if the portal overpowers the visit: cut from the guest’s window glance to a single miniature detail and back to the fixed window frame.

`claim_id CL-H1-01`; `kind: fiction`; `source_id: H1 brief`; `limitations: imagined inn, impossible garden, no floorplan or service evidence`. Address, booking CTA, customer testimony, floorplan accuracy, and service quality: **N/A**. No numerical camera or geometry solve is claimed.

## Original V1 — ORBIT, 14 seconds

**Product SSOT.** `product_id: ORBIT`; `shape: wedge coupe`; `color/mark: single amber side stripe`; `lamps: circular`; `wheels: four, five spokes each`; `logo artwork: verbal identity only`; `must-never-change: wheel count/spokes, stripe continuity, lamp shape, wedge body`. Reserve any exact badge/logo reveal until approved artwork exists.

| Shot | Time | Motion and state |
|---|---:|---|
| SH01 | 0–4 | Ordinary constant-speed roll across shallow wet slate. Vehicle translates relative to fixed ground texture; tracking camera may reduce screen displacement but cannot erase ground travel. Four contact patches and reflections follow it. |
| SH02 | 4–6 | Wheel/side close: wet tread exits contact, small wake trails behind, stripe remains one continuous mark. Cut on the same travel direction. |
| SH03 | 6–10 | Clearly signaled fantasy: vehicle lifts over luminous water. Wheels lose ground contact; no-slip is N/A while airborne. Shadow separates from body; reflection stays tied to water plane. |
| SH04 | 10–12.5 | Descend to wet slate, regain contact, decelerate and stop. Recheck wheel rotation against ground travel after touchdown. |
| SH05 | 12.5–14 | Stationary full vehicle reveal; retained wetness and a settling wake remain plausible. |

**Motion-beat fields.** `initial anchors: slate texture, contact patches, water plane`; `main action: roll → lift → descend → stop`; `contact/transfer: grounded at 0–4 and after touchdown, airborne 6–10`; `secondary motion: wake, droplets, shadow and reflection`; `follow-through: wake settles after stop`. No-slip applies to the ordinary grounded roll and return, subject to visible speed/rotation consistency. It does not govern fantasy levitation. A backward-looking spoke in one still is **not** a QA failure: compare multiple consecutive frames, hub/spoke phase, ground displacement, contact patch, shadow, reflection, camera track, and shutter aliasing. Record a failure only when an interval contradicts the stated motion without a deliberate style cue. Wet residue persists across the lift and return; any reset needs a visible time or surface transition.

`claim_id CL-V1-01`, `kind: fiction`, `limitations: fantasy vehicle; no range, safety, speed, or handling claim`. Uploader model mention is a `DECLARED_UPLOADER_CLAIM`; untimed auto captions are `CAPTION_TEXT_AUTO_UNTIMED`. Audio, source rights, remaster method, and exact logo artwork remain unverified. No real driving directions are conveyed.
